# Mars place notebook

Owner: `mars_place_builder`. Branch `agent/mars-place`. Looks folder `mars-place`. Reports go to `/root` by message.

Read after a compaction: this file, then `AGENTS.md`, `kit/addenda/`, `cut/HANDOVER.md` (Part B, S1 and S2), `assets-src/shared/HANDOVER-NOTES.md` ("How a stop uses it"), `assets-src/shared/STOP-AGENT.md`, `kit/PROGRESS.md` ("Review note of 2026-11-23", "Look lock"), `kit/06-ASSETS.md`, every `RECIPE.md` under `assets-src/mars/`, `kit/07-QUALITY-BAR.md` (G13, R3, R4).

## Brief 238 (from /root, 2026-11-23 09:59 UTC)

Task: S2 of `cut/HANDOVER.md` for Mars. Build the ground on the globe of Mars under the place its dive flies to, so the camera is seen to fly to a real place.

1. Find the twin camera at both handovers (`StopPlan.twin`, `closePose`, `place`) and draw the close side at T as the engine does.
2. Lay that ground back onto the unit sphere from the twin as top down gnomonic plates around the place, at several sizes. Sharp where the close view looks steeply; repaint the stretched parts (image generation only through the skill `media`, at most 6 takes per asset, recipe in `assets-src/mars/RECIPE.md`) or by code. Never a third party picture.
3. Colour match each plate to the level above it at its edge, feather, nest into the patch pyramid as its deepest levels. Rework the levels above so the canyon system seen from the hold leads down to this canyon. Levels come in while small on screen: nothing pops or morphs in view.
4. Result: 2400 to 2454 every frame shows one place coming closer, the place picture's fade in (2442 to 2454) changes nothing a viewer could name. Same in reverse 2700 to 2744. G13 on every frame of dive and rise (at most 0.02 of body pixels under 1.0 texels per pixel).

What may not change: no frame outside the dive (2400 to 2489) and the rise (2670 to 2744) by a single byte. Proof by hash at draft against main for 2212, 2250, 2325, 2399, 2490, 2580, 2669, 2745, 2782, and at final for 2325 and 2580 (sha256 of the PNG equals the first element of `extra.signature` in `out/stills/f02325-mars.hold.jpg.json` and `out/stills/f02580-mars.terrain.jpg.json`). The approved look of the globe and of the close view stays.

Folders: `assets-src/mars/`, `public/assets/mars/`, `src/stops/mars/` except `plan.ts`, `place.json`, `curve.json` (handover_engineer). Engine requests go to `/root/handover_engineer` by message.

Rules: looks every 30 minutes (`npm run looks -- --push --folder mars-place --note "..."`), draft to iterate, review to prove, one render process at a time, values in config or stop JSON, no file over 95 MB, binaries once per milestone, commit trailer `Co-authored-by: Perplexity <noreply@perplexity.ai>`, no em dashes, en dashes, exclamation points or model names.

Milestones: M1 (about 60 minutes): lay back of the close side at T as first plates on the globe, the check picture (globe side with the place picture off beside the close side at T), a 12 frame top down dive sheet from the hold to the twin with no blur. M2: plates repainted, matched, nested, levels above reworked; top down sheet; dense sheets 2430 to 2454 and 2700 to 2724 at draft and across both handovers at review; G13 for every frame of dive and rise; the hash proof.

## Facts from handover_engineer (mail, 10:0x UTC)

- Dump of the place picture on `agent/engine-handover` (commit 08e7cde, not on main yet): `npm run still -- --frame 2454 --tier draft --diag "ungraded/side=orbit/dump=X,Y,image" --out FILE` writes a 1920 x 1080 window of the picture, one picture pixel per pixel, top left at X,Y; values are sRGB of filmic(scene value). `dump=X,Y,alpha` gives the cover (1 ground, 0 sky). The Mars picture is about 4492 x 2668 (scale 2 plus margins), 3 x 3 windows cover it.
- Geometry: `placeLayout` and `placeUv` in `src/core/place.ts`. Twin = `plan.twin(plan.divePose(T, 'terrain'), T)`, T = `handoverOf('mars', 'dive').time` (frame 2454, 81.80833 s; rise frame 2700, 90.00833 s). The body turns 0.9 degrees a second: carry a plate texel to the set frame of T before projecting.
- Heights in radii at draft: 2400 2.42, 2424 0.090, 2436 0.011, 2442 0.0040, 2454 0.00172. One pixel is about height / 930 radii. A 4096 px plate 0.75 degrees wide holds to a height of about 0.003. Frames 2436 to 2443 are a plain blurry red ground today.
- The place as the twin sees it: a wedge from the spot (lon -81.86, lat -4.54) about 3.4 degrees toward east south east (heading -105), plus or minus 50 degrees. The picture's valid field ends 0.06 of the width right of the frame; in rise frames 2709 and 2710 that edge shows as a straight diagonal line.
- Planet shader: patch shown as inverseFilmic(texel) * shade * uTint, then haze, then gain. Mars: limb 0, relief 0.02, reliefNear 0.008; near the ground uTint goes to descent.tint and the haze deepens. All levels share one centre. MAX_PATCHES 6, Mars uses 5. A per level switch "shown as it is" can be asked for.
- Phobos leaves the frame by 2405 in the dive; check 2700 to 2712 in the rise.
- The paths of dive and rise change today (E3): new T frames will be told.

- Body frame: plan local = turn(time) * Qglobe * d. Shader: g = (d.east, d.north) / d.n about the spot, level sampled at g * scale + 0.5, scale = 1 / (2 tan(width / 2)), north at the top. Level weight = front * edge feather 0.12 * (1 - smoothstep(1.02, 1.5, parent texels per pixel)).
- Dive T 81.80833 s frame 2454: twin height 0.00172, vfov 60, about 0.07 degrees behind the spot. Rise T 90.00833 s frame 2700: twin height 0.00100, about 0.11 degrees ahead of the spot. Layout of both pictures: scale 2, left 212, right 116, bottom 119, top 135, 4496 x 2668, picture px = (frame px + left) * 2 + 0.5.
- Heights: dive 2400 2.42, 2412 0.734, 2418 0.257, 2424 0.090, 2430 0.0315, 2436 0.0110, 2442 0.0040, 2448 0.0021, 2454 0.00172. Rise 2700 0.0010, 2706 0.00145, 2712 0.0042, 2718 0.0174, 2724 0.072, 2730 0.30, 2736 1.28, 2742 3.41.
- Predictor: on the hold frames 2250, 2325, 2399 every level has weight 0 (the wrap is denser than 1.5 texels a pixel), so levels cannot change hold bytes. Today's pyramid (96, 48, 24, 12, 3 at 4096) fails G13 when every frame counts: dive 2416 (0.022), 2425 to 2433 (0.02 to 0.11), 2435 to 2453 (0.05 to 0.56); rise 2701 to 2724 (0.64 down to 0.02).
- Rule: a 4096 level of width W holds while height >= 0.228 * W(rad) at vfov 60; a 1920 wide frame needs 2x steps. Ladders tried: `96,48,24,12,6,3,1.5,0.75,0.375` leaves dive 2416 (0.0218) and rise 2701 to 2707 (0.02 to 0.11; the place weight is over 0.5 until about 2706, where the picture counts instead). ### 10:13 to 10:30 UTC, first lay back, the globe's light measured
- Dump stopped after the 9 image windows of the dive (the cover is not needed: the lay back only reads points of the unit sphere below the twin's horizon). The rest (dive cover, rise) can be resumed with dump.sh, it skips files that exist.
- `layback.py` works: the laid back picture rendered on the globe side at T (place off) shows the same mesa and cliff in the same place as the close side. The twin sees a wedge that opens to the east and south east; stretch under 2 only near its tip (3 deg plate: 3 percent of the plate; 0.375 deg plate: 16 percent).
- Encoding: patch textures are sRGB, the globe takes inverseFilmic(linear(texel)) as scene value. The dump is sRGB(filmic(scene)): the same encoding. The ungraded diag is sRGB(clamp(scene, 0, 1)).
- Levels now come from `src/stops/mars/levels.json` (read by assets.ts). For tests it holds 12, 6, 3, 1.5, 0.75, 0.375.
- The globe's own light at T, measured with two flat stills (`flat.sh`, `light.py`): scene G = a * s + h. Light a (b g r) about 0.087 0.124 0.142 over the near and middle ground; haze h (b g r) 0.011 0.014 0.019 at the bottom rows, 0.024 0.031 0.041 mid frame, 0.088 0.111 0.151 near the horizon. So the globe lights the place about 7 times dimmer than the picture's own values, and its haze floor is above the picture's shadows (scene 0.013) on 13 (b), 22 (g), 29 (r) percent of the ground. The plate scene value needed, (c - h) / a: median 0.14 0.085 0.078, 95th percentile 1.9 2.5 5.4, 99th 6 to 10 (the encoding ends at 4.98).
- So albedo alone cannot make the globe show the picture at T. Two ways: the engineer's per level "shown as it is" switch (no shade, no tint; haze stays), or a ground stage in my descent look. Decision below.
- First ratio pass (`calibrate.py`, kept as a tool, superseded by light.py): mean difference globe side to close side on the ground 16.95 of 255, after one ratio pass 15.13.

The rise shrinks the picture by 10 to 15 percent a frame there (u2719 to u2720 scale 0.845; 2714 to 2719 0.598), and the levels are 2.2 to 2.4 apart, so once per level, for one frame, the corner falls outside the level while the level above is still under 1. A level of 4096 px can only be 1.86 times its child (4096 over the frame diagonal 2203) to leave no such corner; mine are 2.2 to 2.4 with no memory to spare. So the margin of 0.001 is set by the rise path's tilt and offset of the place in the frame; if the path changes, G13 must be measured again. Not cured; to say to the engineer and /root.

- handover_engineer (22:31): wants option (i), the globe side holds the ground itself as one material (`handover.scene: "globe"`, the orbit group alone from one eye, no place picture, no dissolve); the cap mesh is in src/bodies/planet.ts (globe_engineer's folder), who writes it is with /root. - My answer to him: the outline is NOT round (a fan to the east of the eye's foot with holes behind ridges; nothing west of the spot but aprons), so it needs a mask in ground coordinates or a test by depth with the globe side lowered a little under the Relief where the mask is 1. What the Relief has that displaced plates cannot: cliff faces (no texels on a steep face from above), texels in perspective, the approved look of 2580. So the Relief stays for the low eye.
- New tool `fillheights.py`: holes take a membrane hung between the seen ground (push and pull through a pyramid); every made point is then put into the picture's eye and lowered (0.05 km a pass) until it lies behind what the eye saw in its pixel; then smoothed from below. MEASURED (1024 px, picture only): 0.7 degrees: 79.5 percent of the plate made, 1.3 percent of it lowered in 11 passes, the most by 0.55 km, 0.03 percent of the made ground still in sight; 1.6 degrees: 78.9 percent made, 1.8 percent lowered in 12 passes, the most by 0.60 km, 0.02 percent still in sight.
- heights.py and fillheights.py got `--picture-only`: the margins round the picture (L0 box x -676 to 6308, y 0 to 3548 against the picture 5632 by 3168) are painted on and drawn out from its border; from above they are stripes across the fan's edge. Left out, the picture's own ground is 20.5 percent of the 0.7 plate and 21.1 percent of the 1.6 plate.
- TRIED AND DROPPED: the pinned depth model (tools/ml/depth.py) on the plates from above: it reads the plate as a plane tilted away to the north (near at the bottom, far at the top), no heights. Look see.jpg.
- New tool `shadeheights.py`: heights from the light in a plate. Brightness (log) is taken as the slope along the Sun's direction on the map and integrated in the frequency domain (wave by wave divided by its wave number along the Sun, held down by 0.35 of the whole wave number so nothing is drawn out along the Sun), waves longer than 0.25 degrees (1.6 plate) or 0.12 (0.7 plate) left to the base. The gain is fitted on the seen ground (picture at its true place against the mesh heights, same band). MEASURED: 1.6 plate (take 6): 76.4 km per unit of log brightness times degree, correlation 0.47 over 198152 texels; 0.7 plate (take 5): 82.1, correlation 0.46 over 200444 texels. The Sun's bearing that fits best is 170 to 190 degrees (correlation 0.42 against 0.40 at 158 and 0.13 at 100): the picture's light comes from the south. Relief made outside the seen ground: 1.6 plate -1.38 to 1.59 km, rms 0.32 km; 0.7 plate -0.90 to 1.35 km, rms 0.23 km.
- SEEN (look c-all): the made heights follow the painted forms (ridges, mesas, hollows stand where the plate shows them), no streaks from one point, no stripes. Still wrong: along the straight north edge of the fan two kinds of ground meet (smooth mesh plateau inside, bumpy made country outside); the canyon ends in a bowl under the eye (the membrane closes it within about 0.1 degrees west of the spot; the plate is painted dark there); the colour of the plates inside the fan is still the flat lay back and must be laid again at true places.

- 2580, the cause as far as read from the code (to be proven by a final with the heading back): the surface set's frame is `f.rotation * turn(time) * terrainFrame` (film.ts state()), so the eye on the ground rides the globe's spin (0.9 degrees a second) and the stars drift across the surface view about the globe's axis as seen from the place. The sky's turn of the surface view is fitted at the start of frame 2580 (film.ts aimSky: cam(frame) at frame / fps), the picture is drawn at the middle of the shutter, so the stars stand a part of a frame's drift from the fitted place, and the turn of the place by 140 degrees (MAIL 478, dive.headingDeg) turns the direction of that drift in the picture. Near bodies (Phobos) are read on the globe side only (film.ts 635), the bend starts at 2611, the plates are not in the surface view: none of them reaches 2580.

- 07:04 FOUND in the first run of the country with aprons (chain 38): the 0.3 degree level had NO seen ground left (relight: 0.0000 of the seen ground) and 0.12 of the plate lowered. CAUSE: country.py's border block found the outside by a flood from texel (0,0); heights.py leaves stray texels of exactly 0 in row 0 of every plate (old files too: about half of row 0), so in every bake so far the flood started on a "seen" texel and marked only that row: the border block (the made relief running on across the picture's field border) had NO effect and nothing was ever lowered under the eye's sight (the old logs say "lowered ... 0.0000 of the plate in 0 passes" at every level). With aprons the texel (0,0) of 1.6, 0.7 and 0.3 degrees is joined to real seen ground and the flood took seen ground for outside. CURE: country.py drops the stray row, floods from a ring round the plate; `plates.json` country.border.on false with onNote, so the made ground is built as in every bake that was looked at. Switching the border block on is a change of its own, not in this commit. The cause of the stray row in heights.py is not found (size 256, most 16 shows none).

report means the next commit within 90 minutes; the third goes into its round as it stands. Looks at least every
30 minutes, the newest 2427, 2469, 2689 and 2726 first. NAMED mail: full sha, branch, own smooth verdict line by
line, what was done per item. Items:
1. The change of view is two scenes (dive 2468 to 2471, rise 2688 to 2690, black wedge lower right 2473, black mass
   lower right until 2692; dive pop 1.80 from 2469, pattern 1.30 from 2467; rise pop 2.12 from 2688 + 1/2, pattern
   1.36 from 2692 + 1/2; step in place 4.85 into 2469, 7.43 into 2689; exchange of relief against a dune plain 2437
   to 2441 and 2707 to 2711). Cause named by /root: the ground of the way down is not the ground of the canyon view.
   Plan F: the relief of the canyon view drawn on the globe as geometry, lit by the same Sun, from the height where
   its tallest thing would shift by a pixel against flat ground, border on the ground, nothing exchanged by height,
   no edge of the relief or of the near plane in the frame at any frame. The cost of a frame is no limit.
2. Fast motion breaks into streaks (2427, 2432 to 2440, 2697 to 2710). My share: ground stretched before it is
   blurred; look with the blur off and say which streaks are in the ground itself.
3. The rise's judged frame 2726: south polar cap pinched, the canyon patch ending at a ruler straight border with
   clamped streaks, storm soft; 2724 to 2744 the disc rolled a quarter turn against the approach 2400 to 2416. The
   rise climbs on the heading and in the light of the dive; the patch feathered into the globe at one sharpness.
4. Detail on the way: G13 red on 27 frames (dive 2440, 2441, 2446 to 2448, 2460 to 2462, 2470, 2471; rise 2685 to
   2689, 2691, 2692, 2702 to 2704, 2708, 2711, 2713, 2716, 2719 to 2721; worst 2687 6.3 percent). 2427: nearest
   ground a featureless wash, haze an airbrushed smudge, pit craters smooth.
5. Not mine: Milky Way, limb rim. Level of 2412 + 1/2 1.32 against 1.3 is mine if my ground makes it. Minor, leave
   and say so: still air 2490 to 2669, soft near cliff of 2580, Phobos at 2325.
6. With take batch 4: smooth.path.minLeave 0.07 (Mars reads 0.008 at 2745 + 1/2); engine gives handover.rise.pullBack.
Rules: my folders only; no write to src/core/warm.json; run npm run smooth -- --stop mars before naming.
